Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The chemistry variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 895 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 385 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 895 + O(1/n^385). Summing over all modes and applying the chemistry convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
